\mbox{\hyperlink{axp20x_8h_a6a08bfde6f11eef315cfbb169fcf6e84}{axp20x\+\_\+device\+\_\+probe()}}\+: Probe a configured axp20x device

@axp20x\+: axp20x device to probe (must be configured)

This function lets the axp20x core register the axp20x mfd devices and irqchip. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} axp20x device passed in must be fully configured with axp20x\+\_\+match\+\_\+device, its irq set, and regmap created. \mbox{\Hypertarget{axp20x_8h_a0402fa8756faa3686a9c9b5d497543b7}\label{axp20x_8h_a0402fa8756faa3686a9c9b5d497543b7}}
